> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-05) — §VM-01-G-FU-a ✅ SHIPPED `56c08f1`: the §CROWN-01 stack moved — seven combat verbs, two verbatim hooks, two once-panels — and the arc's own gift quest turned out to be DEAD TWICE OVER since it shipped.** On "continue" took the board's next-up (1), the only open row needing no design call — the G-FU slice plan's first slice, locked in lab report §11. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `d5e2323`, one claude process, `:1367` stopped throughout (this slice hand-edits inline JS — Hazard #1), and every block read inline in the live file before anything moved. **The plan held for one of its three shapes and was corrected by measurement on the other two.** The seven bare-button dispatches (HW1 kelpie/witch · HG1 mudcrab/octopus · HN1 spawn/demon · INN eel) are `NODE_VERBS` groups `crown-hw1/hg1/hn1/inn` dispatched in place, G4d's shape verbatim, each `narrative`+`combat` with its synthetic battle code (all nine already classified in gate #13 phase 6 — the fence the triage shipped first did its job). But **WG0's gate trial is not a bare D1** — its button lives INSIDE a `.sweelinck-variant` panel (the staged-chain-panel row of §11's own table) — and **the HCA Leviathan is doubly inexpressible**: the iodine burn writes a NUMERIC `iodineBuffBonus = 5|3` and `flag_write` is boolean-only, and the boss button renders visible-but-DISABLED until a held salt burns, which a `when`-gated verb cannot render (absent ≠ disabled). Growing the grammar for one consumer would be the `itemsMinAny` failure, so both moved to **`NODE_HOOKS` verbatim** (G2's method — line-multiset diff, zero unmatched lines) until G4c-FU ask 2 answers the chrome question. The two once-panels (Glut's Gift, Crone Marks) are `NODE_PANELS` entries whose once-guarded text fn carries the verbatim body **including its awards** — stated rule: only legal under `once`. **Finding 1 — the Glut's Gift block was DEAD since it shipped**: its once-guard was `!visited['HG1']`, and `storyCollectLoot` flips `visited[code]` earlier in the same render — the §VM-01-G1-FIX AO class, the exact defect G1 fixed on the HTY panel, sitting one arc further down. The jar was never granted, `quest_glut_06` (gate `flags:['glut_gift_held']`) never listed — **while HG1's own node text says "She gives you the jar as you arrive. … It is warm in your hand."** The prose and the mechanics disagreed and the prose was right. **Finding 2 — the quest was dead a SECOND way: circular completion.** It completes on `flags:['glutGiftReturned']` and the only writer was its own `onComplete`. The fix is the track's own vocabulary: the **`hg1-gift-return` verb** (🍯 Return Glut's Gift to the feeding pool) writes the flag and the quest's own pipeline — onComplete's item_remove/clear/kindness/`glutCrownComplete`, passText — does the rest. **No QUEST_DB edit, no new grammar.** Two designs rejected with reasons recorded: `atNode`+`itemsAll` completion (storyCheckQuests activates AND completes in ONE pass — the jar would grant-and-return in the same arrival, destroying the fiction) and grammar growth for the numeric write. One rule told by a test, not a grep: **the verb's `when` must read the flag its own chain writes** (the la_riva rule) — the driver re-renders BEFORE the end-of-render completion pass, so quest status and inventory are unchanged at that instant. The HW1 `whisperSaintSeen` latch stays inline deliberately: it renders nothing, and the migration moves surfaces. **Evidence:** 26-combo golden HEAD-vs-after over DOM+bbox+msg+overlay+state — **14 byte-identical · 8 id-only (`story-*`→`verb-*`, every bounding box equal) · 4 named deltas, two of which ARE the fix** (hg1-fresh grants the jar + lists The Open Hand; hg1-return-offered shows the new verb) and two the G4d re-render class (a clicked dispatch button returns enabled behind the pre-battle overlay); self-stability 26/26; §7½ **8 story-column PNGs, 6 byte-identical**, the 2 differing being the fix states, both eyeballed; 37-authored-string sweep, every string surviving exactly once; `uqf-node-verbs-crown.test.js` **11/11 with 6 red on HEAD and the 5 greens exactly the verbatim/unchanged-behaviour ones** (G2b's honest shape); three registry pins grown, each right to exist (`uqf-node-hooks` 9→11 · `uqf-npc-row-hooks` npc-row block contiguous but no longer the registry TAIL, the crown stack sits below it in source order · `uqf-node-verbs-d1` label-verb pin excludes `crown-`); `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0**; full Playwright **862 passed / 4 failed** (866 tests — the four are the documented `worldbuilder-crud-arrays` set; also fixed prompt.md §7's baseline chain, where G4d's 851 had never been written in). Docs: lab report **§11a**, `quest.md` §CROWN-01 (the glut_06 row + a dead-twice note — its prose already described the return mechanism the engine never had), `prompt.md` §7 (839→851→862), `anchors:fix`. **Next: G-FU-b §HUNT-01/02 (6 blocks at WRO/BNX/HFT/KSU/ALF/VAW — hooks + panels + 2 combat verbs, no design call) · G-FU-c §ALCHEMY/§WISDOM (the VS shadow block = the second `choice` consumer) · §VM-01-G4c-FU (3 asks) · §VM-01-G2b-FU (design call) · §AUDIT-03d (still the gating row).**

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-04) — §VM-01-G4c ✅ SHIPPED `4c2a831`: the button mode gets its first consumers, and "13 D1 verbs" measured down to 4.** On "continue" took the board's next-up (1), the only row on it needing no design call. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `81908c1`, one other claude process, `:1367` verified **free** the whole session (Hazard #1 — this slice hand-edits inline JS), and the four blocks were read inline in the live file before anything moved. **Shipped:** four D1 surfaces as `NODE_VERBS` button entries — Sweelinck's S49 scene at `NUE`, Ori at `STN`, Yva at `TRD`, Brynn's firewood at `TLL` — plus the Junction Vignette's `[Help — 10gp]` converted **in place** to the `cost` leaf through a new `_uqfRunChain(bits)` (no mount, no re-render, `storyBlock` on a refusal), because that surface's lines are drawn once per node by `JUNCTION_VIGNETTES` and a re-render would replace them with *"…has moved on"*. That is the G4 design pass's **stated bar for `cost` not being single-use**, and it is met: two live consumers, two prices, two surfaces. **The finding is the count, and it is why this row is not the whole of G4c.** The lab report listed **13** D1 surfaces and sliced them as one unit. Read against the *shipped grammar* rather than against the census, **only 4 are expressible today**; the other 9 are each blocked by a shape the VM does not have — **a delayed second beat** (Harbor Board · Solvak probe · Seal delivery each end `setTimeout(() => storyMsg(…), 400–800)`, and since `storyMsg` **replaces** the single message slot, the first line is *authored to be destroyed*; `narrative` carries no timing and the kernel is pure, so it cannot schedule), **panel chrome** (Surge Lock · Froberger letter · Codex Inquisitor each hand-build a `.sweelinck-variant` with six independent style knobs — a byte-identical generic panel would be a CSS struct smuggled into a vocabulary), and **a ceremonia launch** (the Inquisitor's button *starts* a check, which is exactly why the `skill_check` scope fence needs no widening — but no bit starts one). Answering any of those inside this slice would be the `itemsMinAny` failure the track exists to prevent, so all three are filed as **§VM-01-G4c-FU** with a recommendation each. **Three schema growths, each with a measured reason:** **`group`** names a *dispatch position* — four blocks in four source positions cannot share one call site, and LIFO stacking is preserved only by dispatching where the block sat; it is §9.1's own D3 word, so G4d inherits it, and **every group has exactly one call site and every call site names a live group, asserted**, because an entry with no call site renders **nowhere** (§PLAY-01-G's silent rot, in a new place). **`bits` may be `fn(st)`** — the same `string | fn` shape `ambient` and `when` already have in this registry, for the two sites whose narrative the inline handler assembled at click (Yva gains a paragraph once the Harrow is solved; `_getS49SweelinckScene()` closes differently on NG+); the VM's grammar is untouched, `execBits` still receives a plain declarative array. **`btnStyle`, and a label-only verb IS its button — no wrapper**, which is the one the *evidence* caught rather than the design: the migrated blocks inserted a **bare** button afterend of `#story-text-box`, whose parent is a **flex column**, so a direct-child button is stretched to full width while the identical button inside a mount shrinks to its own text. **The golden-DOM capture reported that as identical** — same tag, class, label, style — **and the §7½ screenshot showed a full-width bar on one side and a small left-aligned chip on the other.** *A DOM diff proves markup, not layout*; the bounding box is part of the capture now. One correction to the design sketch: it wrote `{kind:'favor', npc:'yva', add:1}`, but `_setNpcFavor` writes an **absolute** level and only ever raises, so `add:1` would have **lowered** a favor already at 2 — shipped as `set:1`, pinned by a test. **Evidence, in the order it was built.** (a) A **23-combo golden capture, HEAD vs after**, over DOM markup *and* bounding box, message text, message class, gold, hp, inventory, favor, quests and flags: **11 byte-identical · 5 differ ONLY by the `id` the button now carries** (every "offered" state plus the unaffordable refusal — box included, so the four migrated surfaces are **pixel-identical**) **· 7 carry one delta, and it is G4b's, already named**: the driver re-renders when a chain ends, so a quest the verb satisfies **completes in the same beat** rather than on the next arrival. It is paid **exactly once** either way — §VM-01-G3 moved those payouts into the quests' own `onComplete` for precisely this reason — and the migration *recovers* two narratives the inline handlers destroyed (`_setNpcFavor`'s `🤝 …looks at you differently now.` used to overwrite Yva's entire speech; `storyCheckQuests`' own messages used to overwrite the firewood's). All four DUS states and all three junction states are byte-identical, including the `msg-block` shake class on a refused price — which is what proves the price conversion is a no-op and not a rewrite. (b) **§7½ eyeballed**: 9 screenshots of the live game, **6 byte-identical PNGs**, the 3 that differ being the click outcomes; this is where the flex-column layout bug was found, *after* the DOM diff had said "identical". (c) **`uqf-node-verbs-d1.test.js` 13/13, with 7 of 13 failing at HEAD and the 6 that pass exactly the ones asserting *unchanged* behaviour** (Yva's full chain, the favor floor, Sweelinck's NG+ closing, both junction states) — G2b's honest shape rather than G4b's all-red one, because most of this surface is supposed to be identical. Plus `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0** with `check:questparity` at **25,030 bytes — the kernel was not touched again**; full Playwright **839 passed / 4 failed** (the run reported 838/5 and the fifth was the known `multiplayer-presence:171` flake, which passes **12/12** on its own). **Two pins updated, and both were right to exist:** `uqf-node-verbs.test.js`'s *"the DUS trio is the whole table"* becomes *"the head of the table"*, and **`uqf-quest-activation.test.js:120`, which pinned that clicking Ori paid *nothing at click time*** — a timing artifact of the inline handler, not the property the test was written for. The property is *"+300gp exactly once, from the quest chain"*, and it is now asserted against a **further** render. **Pin the property, never the incident.** Docs: lab report **§12⅞** + 2 new anchors (gate #15 caught the old `_renderNodeVerbs` anchor as a **dead symbol** the moment its signature grew `group` — exactly what a symbol anchor is for), `quest.md` §"the button verb", `prompt.md` §4 + §7 (baseline 826→**839**), `anchors:fix`. **Next: G4d** — CDG's D3 concurrent menu as grouped `NODE_VERBS` with `combat` bits, the two Class-E strays (Kenickie's market, the Lower Archive launcher) to `NODE_HOOKS` verbatim, and the la_riva delivery that shares their container. Then **§VM-01-G4c-FU**, once its three asks are answered.
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-04) — §VM-01-G4b ✅ SHIPPED `f7a60a5`: the first `choice` in the game's history, and the schema grew one mode because a choice has no button.** On "continue" took the board's next-up (1), the only row on it needing no design call. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `5d64e89`, one other claude process, `:1367` verified **free** the whole session (Hazard #1 — this slice hand-edits inline JS), and `grep -c NODE_VERBS` returned **0** in both `play.html` and `src/js/quest.js`, so the row's premise held. **Shipped:** `NODE_VERBS` + `_renderNodeVerbs` — the third small vocabulary beside `NODE_PANELS` (flavour a node *shows*) and `NODE_HOOKS` (an interface a node *owns*) — and Kern & Sable's **76 inline lines** at DUS as its three entries (Q-NEXUS-00/01/02, the Torment Nexus overture). **The design finding is that §9.1's locked schema was for the wrong half of Class D.** The lab report locked `{id, nodes, when, label, bits}` — a **button** verb, which is the D1 shape it was measured against. A **D2 exclusive choice has no button**: the options *are* the surface. Giving Kern & Sable a `label` would have inserted a click the player never had to make, so the entry's mode is decided by what it carries — **`label`+`bits`** = a button that runs the chain on click · **`bits` with no `label`** = the chain IS the surface, so it runs at render and its first bit **must** park on a `choice` · **`ambient`** alone = a flavour line for a state with nothing to do. That third mode is where the slice earned its keep: running a chain at render is exactly how side effects land on every draw of a node, so a label-less non-suspending chain is refused with a warning — **and refused BEFORE its mount is created.** The first implementation refused *after* inserting the mount and the test caught it: **a refusal that leaves an empty div behind is not a refusal, it is a page that changed anyway.** **Two behaviour deltas, both named in the diff rather than discovered in play:** (1) the driver re-renders when a chain ends, so the Q-NEXUS-01 follow-up now appears **in the same beat** as [Ask] instead of on the next arrival, and the aftermath line lands the moment Q-NEXUS-02 completes — the fiction supports it (they are still at the counter, waiting), it is asserted in the test file, and `story.md`'s *"next `DUS` visit"* wording is **kept and annotated as authored intent** rather than rewritten; (2) the **empty 8px spacer div** the inline block always inserted — visible as dead space in the "listened and walked away" state — is gone. **Evidence, in the order it was built.** (a) An **authored-string multiset diff** between HEAD's block and the new table: **14 vs 14, identical, nothing added or lost**, with the three ambient lines unwrapped from the `innerHTML` wrapper the old code hand-built. This is G2b's line-multiset method adapted to a block that **could not** move verbatim — its shape changed, so only its content can be pinned, and pinning the content is what proves no prose was retyped from a screenshot. (b) A **golden-DOM diff of all four DUS states** against HEAD (temp harness, deleted): text and button labels **byte-identical, including the 6px/4px button spacing** — `_uqfRenderAsk` was given the first-button 6px that every hand-written option panel in the file uses, so the migration is pixel-identical rather than merely equivalent. What remains is the mount's `id`, a `data-uqf-option` attribute, one redundant wrapper `<div>` + `<em>` dropped from an already-italic `.npc-ambient` (CSS 2122 — zero pixels), and delta 2. (c) **`uqf-node-verbs.test.js` 10/10, with all 10 failing at HEAD.** That is deliberately **not** G2b's "some tests pass both ways" shape, and saying so is the honest part: the surface's DOM identity changed with the migration, so the before/after equivalence has to live in the golden diff, not in the after-state contract — and the test file says that in its own header. Plus `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0** with `check:questparity` at **25,030 bytes — the kernel was not touched**, this slice is entirely host code (G4a spent the track's one parity-fence edit); full Playwright **826 passed / 4 failed** = the documented `worldbuilder-crud-arrays` four; and **§7½ eyeballed** in the running game — all three states screenshotted, the sidebar ITEMS counter ticking **0→1** as the token lands through `reward`, and the newline-collapsing in `#story-move-msg` checked against HEAD and confirmed **pre-existing** (`storyMsg` sets `textContent` on an element with no `pre-wrap`, before and after). One thing factored rather than duplicated: `_mkAmbientLine` is now the single definition a `choice` prompt and a `NODE_VERBS` `ambient` both use, so they cannot drift. Docs: lab report **§12¾** + 5 new anchors, `quest.md` §"`NODE_VERBS` — where a verb is authored", `story.md` §Q-NEXUS + `world.md` annotated, `prompt.md` §4 + §7 (baseline 816→**826**), `anchors:fix` for the 113 hints my +39 net HTML lines drifted. **Next: G4c** — the 13 D1 verbs → `NODE_VERBS` (the button mode now has a registry to land in) plus converting the Junction Vignette `[Help — 10gp]` to `cost`, which is the parent's stated bar for the leaf not being single-use. Then G4d (CDG's concurrent menu + the two Class-E strays → `NODE_HOOKS`).
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-04) — §VM-01-G4a ✅ SHIPPED `b905733`: the `choice` opcode has a host end at last, and the `cost` leaf says the price out loud.** On "continue" the user answered the one ASK the G4 design lock was waiting on — **(a) refuse-at-click**, the recommendation — which unblocked the only row on the whole board that needed no further design call. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `32845cd`, `:1367` verified free the whole session (Hazard #1 — this slice hand-edits inline JS), and greps for `_uqfRunVerb` / `NODE_VERBS` / `cost` returned **nothing**, so the row's premise held. **Zero content moved, exactly as §10 sliced it** — G4a is the seam, G4b is its first consumer. **Shipped, kernel side (`src/js/quest.js`, inside QUEST:CORE → re-inlined, parity green at 25,030 bytes):** the **`cost`** opcode, registered next to `reward` because it is `reward`'s inverse and because `reward` is precisely how you get this wrong — `gold:-50` computes correctly with no affordability test, no refusal and the word *reward* on a price, Hazard #2's *a write into a real-but-wrong object never throws*. Contract `{gold?, resource?, count?, refuse?}`; **every currency is tested before any is spent**, so a mixed price can never part-pay — a property the design report measured toward but never named, and the one a naive leaf breaks silently. The leaf needed a mechanism the VM did not have — **chain failure**: a handler may set `ctx._halt` and `execBits` breaks on it. It is **deliberately never cleared in the loop**, because `ctx` is shared with the nested `execBits` a `choice` option runs: clearing it would let the bits *after* an unpaid branch run on. One `ctx` per run is what makes a sticky flag safe, and both drivers build a fresh one. **Shipped, host side (outside the fence, beside `_uqfPump`):** `_uqfRunVerb`/`_uqfRenderAsk` — the half Inc A never wrote. An `{ask:'choice'}` envelope now renders one button per option into the verb's own mount, resumes the **same** generator with the picked index, and on completion removes the mount and re-renders the node with the chain's narrative as `storyRender`'s **prefix** (`_resolveQuestUQF`'s route — the tail `storyMsg` would otherwise overwrite it, §BOARD-01-FU6). Two properties designed in rather than discovered: `storyRender` **abandons** a pending generator beside the sweep that takes its panel's DOM (Inc A's single slot assumed *"a choice resolves within one interaction turn"*; a NODE choice can sit across a render — and abandoning writes nothing, because `choice` applies the picked option's bits only *after* the pick), and a stale option button **re-checks that the slot still holds its own generator**, so a click on a survived panel is inert rather than resuming a dropped chain. **The scope fence is untouched:** a `choice` inside `skill_check`'s `onPass`/`onFail` still throws, so a `require('./quest')` server still needs no host driver. **Evidence:** new `uqf-verb-driver.test.js` **12/12**, and **the positive control is the shape that matters — against HEAD 10 of 12 fail, and the 2 that pass are exactly the two asserting *unchanged* behaviour** (a chain with no `cost` runs every bit; a `choice` in a skill-check branch still throws). `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0** (`check:questparity` byte-identical both ways). Full Playwright **816 passed / 4 failed** = the documented `worldbuilder-crud-arrays` four. **The first run reported 815/5, and the fifth red was mine and was correct**: `quest-runtime-uqf.test.js:28` pins the entire `HANDLERS` key list precisely so a new opcode cannot enter the table unrecorded — adding `cost` to the pin is part of the fix, not a concession to it, and it is the fourth time this repo has been told something by a test rather than by a grep. **No §7½** — nothing player-visible changed, because nothing calls `_uqfRunVerb` yet and `cost` has no consumer until G4c; the whole point of the slice is that it is provable before a single block moves. Docs: lab report **§12½ addendum** (the ASK answered + what shipped), `prompt.md` §4 (the `cost` + driver contracts, incl. *`cost` never belongs in a `when`*) and §7 (baseline 804→**816**), **`quest.md` — a new "Prices and choices" section** so an author reaching for a price finds the leaf instead of writing the three inline lines a sixth time, and `anchors:fix` for the 113 hints my +115 HTML lines drifted. **Next: G4b — Kern & Sable, the first `choice` in the game's history, shipping alone so it can be eyeballed (§7½) without 13 migrations in the same diff.** Then G4c (13 D1 verbs → `NODE_VERBS` + the Junction Vignette `cost` conversion, which proves the leaf isn't single-use) → G4d.
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-04) — §VM-01-G4 CHILD LAB REPORT ⚠️ DESIGN LOCKED PENDING ONE ASK `f340143` (the ASK is now ANSWERED — see NEWEST): the `choice` opcode Inc A shipped has no host end, and only a third of "Class D" is a choice at all.** On "continue" took the board's next-up (1) — the last migration slice, whose stated prerequisite was its own child design pass (the §VM-01-G lock: *"G4 OK in principle, but G4 gets its own child design pass first"*). Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `ca0113c`, **one** claude process, `:1367` verified free throughout, no `lab-report-vm01g4-*` existed, and every number re-measured live rather than carried from the parent. **The re-measurement is itself the track's evidence:** the region is **1,006 lines — 41% of the parent's 2,445** (front 33332→**33702**, in-region `node.code ===` 77→**29**), because G1+G2+G2b+G3 moved the front **forward 1,439 lines in seven days**. The parent's *"the front moves backward ~4/day"* is no longer true inside it. **Census: 22 blocks / 838 classified lines**, read end to end, not sampled. **Finding 1 — the keystone has no host end, and this reorders the whole slice.** Inc A built the coroutine correctly (`*execBits@22224`, the suspending `*choice@22320`, `_uqfPump@6827`, `_uqfPending@6824`) and the half that **renders the ask and resumes with an answer was never written**: `_uqfPump` has **exactly one** caller in the HTML — `_uqfRunToCompletion@6840`, which **throws** on an unresolved ask — **all four** live `execBits` entry points wrap in it (`6880`/`6883`/`30017`/`36843`), the fifth carries a comment saying *"a choice in onPass/onFail throws, by scope-fence"*, `_uqfPending` is **read by nothing** outside two integration tests, and **`renderChoiceBlock` occurs 0 times in `play.html` and 0 times in `src/js/quest.js`** — it has never existed as code. §DX-01e-FU2 reached that from the anchor side; this reaches it from the call graph. **So a `choice` bit in any live quest today does not render a choice — it throws**, and G4 is `choice`'s first **HOST** consumer, not just its first content consumer. **G4a is therefore the driver, shipping with zero content migrated** — provable against the existing coroutine tests before one player-facing block moves. **Finding 2 — "Class D" is three shapes and `choice` fits one of them.** The parent's description fits the *click handler* of all 22 blocks and the *surface* of very few. Of **21 button surfaces: 13 are SINGLE VERBS** (one button, no alternative — a one-option choice is not a choice, it is a labelled effect chain with ceremony), **7 are exclusive CHOICES**, and **CDG's `cq-boss-buttons` is a CONCURRENT menu** whose Taz/Don/Cat-King confrontations are visible at once by design — mapping those onto an opcode that is *exclusive by construction* would be an outright **behaviour change**. So the vocabulary is **`NODE_VERBS {id, node, when, label, bits}`** in the `NODE_PANELS`/`NODE_HOOKS` house style (ordered registry, in-place dispatch → LIFO preserved by construction), with `choice` as a **bit inside it**, used only where the block is genuinely exclusive. Forcing one tool onto all of Class D would be the parent's own warning — *"this is how `itemsMinAny` happened, a shape grown for the wrong layer"* — coming true inside the slice written to honour it. **Grammar gap, measured rather than assumed:** the parent's *"`cost` leaf (gold≥N, consume) — ONE new opcode"* is half already shipped — **`item_remove@22303` covers every consume site** — and the missing half is **three currencies**: gold at **six** sites (Yva 50gp · the Junction Vignette `[Help — 10gp]`, which is the second consumer that proves the leaf isn't single-use · Kenickie's four prices), `surgeCharges` (spent permanently), and hp — and **hp is NOT a price**: the Memory Gate's −15 is narrated damage on a branch that always succeeds, so it belongs in that option's bits. Two shipped properties a naive leaf would silently change: **affordability is tested at CLICK, not at render** at every gold site, and **`reward` with negative gold "works"** arithmetically with no affordability test and the word *reward* on a price — Hazard #2's *a write into a real-but-wrong object never throws*. **Deliberately not migrated, each with its reason recorded so it doesn't read as an oversight:** Entry 42 (`S_story.entry42Text@34647`) and the ZRH void-toll rune (`S_story._voidTollSecret@34769`) capture **free text**, which `choice` cannot express *by design* (its resume value is an index *"so the data author never couples to presentation"*) and which a `prompt_text` ask for two sites would make the single-use term the Host/Script Separation Policy forbids — **grow it at three consumers, not two**; **Kenickie's Black Market (71 lines) and the Lower Archive launcher are Class E sitting in G4's region** and belong to `NODE_HOOKS` by G2's verbatim method — they fell between three slices (G2 deferred them, G2b found Kenickie *"is a CDG surface in G3's territory"*, G3 migrated activation not UI) and are recorded rather than dropped a fourth time; the Reading Circle is a **day-cooldown counter** whose repeat notion already lives in quests (`retryable`/`retryGateDays`). **The §VM-01-G2b act-leg thread is now closed for this region:** one comparison survives — `(S_story.actNumber || 1) >= 3@34678` at NUE — and **NUE is `act:6`, so it is vacuously TRUE, not dead**. Nothing to fix and nothing to file; deleting it would be an unverified rewrite of authored intent (§AUDIT-03m-FU). Recorded so the class is measured closed rather than assumed. **Slices locked: G4a** driver + `cost` (the only parity-fence edit in the track — `src/js/quest.js`, re-inline, `check:questparity`) · **G4b** Kern & Sable as the `choice` pilot (76 lines; no cost, no combat, no free text, one node — so the first choice in the game's history ships alone and can be eyeballed) · **G4c** the 13 D1 verbs → `NODE_VERBS` + convert the Junction Vignette Help to `cost` · **G4d** CDG's menu (as `combat` bits — **`combat`'s optional `nodeCode`@22135 already accepts the synthetic `CQ_TAZ` code those buttons use**) + the two Class-E strays. **Evidence:** `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0**; `check:anchors` **129 symbol anchors across 7 docs all resolve, 0 stale** — the lab report is the 7th doc and added 30 anchors, each verified individually, and **4 hints were off by one and were corrected by hand rather than left for `anchors:fix` to guess** (§DX-01e-FU: `--fix` picks the NEAREST hit). Docs only — **no HTML, no tests, no §7½**, Playwright untouched so the **804/4** baseline stands. **🟡 THE ONE ASK, and it blocks G4a:** when a verb is unaffordable, keep **refuse-at-click** (today's behaviour at all six gold sites, byte-identical, consistent with the track's telling-vs-asking thesis — **my recommendation**) or **gate at render** (tidier UI, but a real behaviour change at six sites and a quieter world)? The `cost` leaf's contract differs between the two, so G4a should not start until it is answered. **Next: answer the ASK → G4a; or §VM-01-G2b-FU / §DX-02m / §AUDIT-03d, all still waiting on their own design calls.**
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-08-04) — §VM-01-G2b ✅ SHIPPED `bedf2c1`: the ctx design that blocked this slice was ONE FIELD — and moving the 29 blocks read five narrative beats out of the game that can never fire.** On "continue" with every §AUDIT-03 row still waiting on a user design call, took the active engineering track's open slice — the one whose blocker was an *engineering* decision, not a user one. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `bedb19a`, one other claude process, WBAPI **stopped** throughout (`:1367` verified free before and after — Hazard #1, this slice hand-edits inline JS). **The blocker was answered by measurement, not design.** G2 deferred these because they "need a ctx-argument design"; of the **89** `storyRender` locals in scope at the region, the 29 blocks read **exactly one** — `npcRowDiv`. The scan's apparent `n`/`row`/`txt`/`fav` hits were `\n` escapes inside strings, a word inside a comment, and block-local declarations, and the two candidates anyone would expect — `keys` and `_npcNodeKey` — are read by **none** of them. So the contract is `{ npcRowDiv }`, built once beside the anchor it carries; `_runNodeHook(id, node, ctx)` forwards it; **21** hooks destructure it in the signature and **8** take `(node)` alone and are asserted never to name the anchor. **Two safety properties checked BEFORE the move, because they are what makes a verbatim move legitimate:** no **top-level `return`** (all 13 sit inside click handlers — one at block level would have gone from exiting nothing to exiting a hook early) and no **`var`** (nothing hoists out of one block for a later one to read). **The single piece of evidence I would keep if I could keep only one: a line-multiset diff of the two revisions — nothing was removed except the 2 old `_runNodeHook` lines**, and every added line is scaffolding (29 fn headers, 29 closers, 29 registry entries, 29 dispatch calls, 13 comments, the ctx). All 895 moved lines are byte-identical; they were not even re-indented. **Behavioural proof: a 17-combo golden-DOM/state diff, 16 byte-identical.** The 17th (`HKG`) differed — and **differed again between two runs of the SAME code**, because the CY Madness Gate draws unseeded `Math.random()` into two *persisted* `S_story` fields. That is a pre-existing invariant-#6 violation, not a regression, and the after-vs-after run is what proves it rather than my say-so; filed as **§DX-02m** (51 sites; `check:rng` guards stream parity, so nothing gates *which* stream a state write draws from). **The finding: five act-gated beats in this region cannot fire.** `storyRender` sets `S_story.actNumber = node.act || 1` on every render, before the region, and every Birka node is `act:1` — so the lamp inquiry (`>=2`), the Heartwood letter (`>=4`), Yael's Named Report (`>=6`), the S54 joint witness (`>=7`) and the Act VIII Quill farewell (`===8`) are unreachable. **It cascades**: `birka-lamp-choice` waits on `brynnKeeperStoryTold`, and the dead Beat 1 is its only writer, so the §XXXV vigil arc past its ambient line is dead too. Same structurally-dead act leg **§VM-01-G3** retired from the quest stanzas — the third slice of this track to find that special-case code keyed on node state rots silently, which is the row's whole thesis. **Filed, not fixed**: G3 could drop its act legs because the other gate leaves already carried the staging; here the act leg *is* the staging, so something must replace it — and `world.md` §XXXIX designs Yael's scene for `LLA`/`HKG` at Act IV+ while the code fires at `LHR` at Act VI+, so neither number is trustworthy without corroboration (§AUDIT-03m-FU's laundering lesson). **§VM-01-G2b-FU** carries it, `world.md` + `story.md` are **annotated at all five sites** (annotate, never rewrite), and a test pins all five gates plus the cascade so the fix has to update it. **Evidence:** `check:walk` **16/16 exit 0** · new `uqf-npc-row-hooks.test.js` **8/8** with a **positive control** — against HEAD **5 of 8 fail**, and the 3 that pass are exactly the live-behaviour ones, which is the right shape: those test the game, not the migration · `uqf-node-hooks` 4/4 (its registry assertion now owns the G2 head explicitly) · full Playwright **804 passed / 4 failed** = the documented `worldbuilder-crud-arrays` four, with the known `multiplayer-presence:171` flake green 7/7 in isolation · inline `<script>` parses clean. **The `npcRowDiv` anchor class is now empty** (the only remaining references are the element's own construction, the ctx and the final insert), and G2's deferral list was approximate: **Kern & Sable is not an npc-row block** and **Kenickie** is a CDG surface in G3's territory — both recorded rather than quietly dropped. Docs: lab report §10, `prompt.md` §7 baseline 796→804, `anchors:fix` for the 7 hints my +140 lines drifted. **Next: §VM-01-G4 (needs its own child lab report — the last migration slice) or the G-FU engine-region tail; every open §AUDIT-03/§DX-02 row still needs the user's design call.**
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-07-31) — §DX-02g ✅ SHIPPED `f229ede`: two monsters carried a tier the engine has no entry for, and all four readers of that field answered with a wrong number instead of an error.** On "continue" with §AUDIT-03d's design question still unanswered, took the row the last board named first among the no-design-call alternatives. Existing-Work-First: tree clean at `fe3c14c`, server stopped, a 2nd claude process present but the game file untouched. **The premise held; the row's framing did not, and re-measuring before editing is what caught it.** The row called this *"an authoring-surface defect, not a gameplay one"* — but `tier` is read in six places and **every reader falls back rather than throwing**: `_voidEnrage` → `|| {atk:1,dmg:1,die:0}`, so The Warden (which matches `_VOID_ENEMY_RE`) ran its §PLAY-01-B low-HP press at the **weakest magnitude in the game** on a sealed Act-5 guardian; `_storyRollInit` → initiative `+0` instead of `+3`; `_weightedMonsterPick` → a flat `|| 10`, and because `_inferTerrain` answers with a *neighbouring node's* terrain, cells beside **TBS** infer `epic_goblin_cave` — so Kazrath drew as a **random wilderness encounter at 2.5× a `hard` monster's weight** at low notoriety; and both threat badges printed the literal **`RARE`** while asking for a `.threat-rare` CSS rule **that does not exist**. **The tiers were derived, not tasted:** `void_rat_swarm → easy` settled itself (6 of 8 nearest stat-neighbours `easy`; exact AC/HP/atk twin is Jackalwere), while `void_shaman` was a real fork — its two **exact** stat-block twins are both `hard`, but `_monsterLevel` puts it at 6, a level that is 43-medium / 4-hard — so it was **asked with both readings measured, and `hard` was chosen**. **Shipped:** two `./api.sh put monster … tier=` calls, nothing else in the HTML. **Evidence:** `git diff` **exactly 2 lines**; **golden parse diff across all 7 collections = exactly 2 entry diffs, both `fields=tier` alone**, 0 lost / 0 gained; new `dx02g-monster-tier-contract.test.js` **8/8** with **5 of the 8 failing on the real pre-fix file** (restored byte-identical; the 3 that pass there are the ones already true — neither half is vacuous); `check:walk` **14/14 exit 0**; full Playwright **740 passed / 4 failed** = the documented crud-arrays four, nothing new. **§7½ DONE:** the Void Shaman's Sanctum pre-battle overlay now shows a styled orange **HARD** badge where it used to print an unstyled `RARE`; 0 page errors. **The durable half is the inverse assertion** — the test pins that **no tier-keyed engine map ships partial** (all 8 tier literals + the 6 notoriety weight rows + the 5 `.threat-*` CSS rules must cover all five), because a map missing a tier is the identical silent-fallback defect from the other direction. **The lesson, and it is §DX-01c's in a third dimension: a lookup that falls back never throws — so "no error" is not evidence the value is known.** Docs: `monsters.md` gained the tier-contract section with the six-reader table, and its stale `del monster` warning (fixed 2026-07-30) was retired in the same pass. **Side findings filed, not swept: §DX-02h** (`void_rat_swarm` has exactly 2 references in the file — no roster, no battle, no quest; it is unreachable content) and **§DX-02i** (`XP_BY_TIER` is declared and read by nothing, and it encodes a *different* XP model than the one that ships). **Next: §AUDIT-03d (still the gating row — needs the staging design call; it releases 983 quests), else §AUDIT-03o (measured, but a design call), §DX-01e (line-anchor drift — the last open §DX-01 row), §DX-02h/i (both small, both filed today), §AUDIT-03i/k/m, or §VM-01-G2b / the G4 child report.**
>

> **⟶ PRIOR (2026-07-28) — §VM-01-G3 SHIPPED: Class-C quest activation is declarative — and the silent-rot thesis proved out three more times.** On "continue" took G3 (the unambiguous next slice; G2b/G4 need design calls). Branch `feat/vm-01-g3`. **Grep-before-building rewrote the row's premise before any edit:** (1) most of the 15 quests ALREADY carried an `activateNode` — appended as a DUPLICATE key below the authored `activateNode:null` by the `a721254`/`ea02faf` audit waves (last-key-wins), so the cat chain + tl_02/03 + vs_02/03/warden + la_riva_02/03 mass-activated with vacuous `gate:{}` and the legacy stanzas' staging was already dead; (2) `710bb75`'s SF→LCY remap sent tl_01/tl_03 to the wrong city (SF = Storefront = STN); (3) `actNumber` = `node.act` (constant per node), so every "act ≥ N" activation leg was structurally dead — no `actMin` leaf needed, `countMin`/`questsDone`/`notFlags` express everything; (4) **`NODE_MAP.VS` + `NODE_MAP.TL` had no `code` field — the entire §XX Visby arc and the §XIX Vonn path (tl_02's only completion) had NEVER run in live play.** **Shipped:** real gates on 20 quests (15 migrated + 5 adjacent dedup/gating), `onActivate` field (absent → 📋 · null → silent · {msg,delayMs} → the staged Jimmy/Sandy/Tommy/Isolde narrations verbatim), `boardExempt` on the NG+ set, `_uqfActivateAtNode` extracted + run at storyRender START (same-arrival availability for status-keyed UI — the Solvak-button lesson), VS/TL `code` fixed via `./api.sh put node`, the revived Ori + seal-delivery buttons de-double-paid (narrate + set the completion flag; the quest's onComplete pays once, keyed on quest-active), Yva's button re-keyed on `quest_vs_02` active (heals the FU6 referral dead-end), FU6 legitimacy tests moved to in-sequence-at-referral-time. **Evidence:** 20-combo pre/post golden-state harness (temp, deleted) **14 byte-identical / 6 intended / 0 unexpected**; permanent `uqf-quest-activation.test.js` **8/8**; `quest-runtime-uqf` 303/303 + warrants-board 25/25 + npc-card-map 22/22 + panels/hooks/gate-ast/coroutine/env/softlock/quest-core + 4 smokes = **410 green in the final runs**;
